<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前量子硬件在规模、噪声和保真度上仍有限制，且在物理机器上运行量子算法成本高昂。大多数高性能量子电路模拟器提供GPU后端，但很少原生支持NPU，这限制了量子算法研究的计算平台选择。

Method: Aicir是一个完整栈的量子电路模拟器，通过单一编程模型连接电路构建、多种状态表示、测量、微分、变分算法、量子机器学习和量子架构搜索。它使用配对实张量、固定秩门视图和硬件特定公式来保持NPU上的模拟路径在设备内执行。该表示还允许将状态分割到2^p个NPU上，同时保留反向模式微分。

Result: Aicir在禁用CPU回退的情况下验证了NPU原生执行，并在2、4、8个NPU上检查了分布式通信和梯度。对于测试的融合分层电路，Aicir的CPU运行时为Qiskit Aer的0.97-1.28倍、Cirq的0.76-1.10倍，与现有模拟器处于同一水平；NPU测试则确立了正确的原生执行。

Conclusion: Aicir的CPU执行性能与成熟模拟器相当，同时提供了原生NPU支持这一差异化优势。NPU测试证明了正确性而非CPU到NPU的加速比提升，表明该工作填补了量子电路模拟器缺乏NPU后端的空白。

Abstract: Quantum computing is a promising way to study problems that are difficult for classical methods, but current quantum hardware still faces limits in scale, noise, and fidelity. Running quantum algorithms on physical machines can also be costly. Quantum circuit simulators therefore remain important because they let researchers design and test algorithms on classical computers before using quantum hardware. Most high-performance simulators provide GPU backends, while few offer native support for NPUs. This gap limits the computing platforms available for quantum-algorithm research. We developed Aicir to provide a full-stack quantum circuit simulator with a native Huawei Ascend NPU backend. Aicir connects circuit construction, several state representations, measurement, differentiation, variational algorithms, quantum machine learning, and quantum architecture search through one programming model. It also supports noise simulation, tensor-network and matrix-product-state engines, and distributed state simulation. On the NPU, paired real tensors, fixed-rank gate views, and hardware-specific formulas keep the tested simulation paths on the device. The same representation lets Aicir partition a state across $2^{p}$ NPUs while retaining reverse-mode differentiation. We validated native execution with CPU fallback disabled and checked distributed communication and gradients on 2, 4, and 8 NPUs. For the tested fused layered circuits, Aicir's CPU runtime is within $0.97$--$1.28\times$ that of Qiskit Aer and $0.76$--$1.10\times$ that of Cirq. These results place its CPU execution in the same range as established simulators for this workload, while the NPU tests establish correct native execution rather than CPU-to-NPU speedup.

</details>


### [95] [Quantized Stabilizer-Rényi Boundary Response across Fermionic SPT Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09749)
*M. A. Rajabpour*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了开边界系统中受拓扑保护的Majorana模在stabilizer Rényi熵上的印记。在Rényi指数α=1/2处，作者通过精确的有限开链关系、大L的Pfaffian计算和正权重检验，在自由费米子BDI链中隔离出体态扣除后的边界响应。该响应对应于|Δω|ln 2（Δω为绕数变化），能够计数改变费米子SPT指标的Majorana通道数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开边界承载着对称性保护的Majorana模，但其在stabilizer Rényi熵上的特征被非普遍的体积律所掩盖。作者希望找到一种方法从体积律背景中提取出边界贡献，从而利用stabilizer Rényi熵来表征拓扑相变和SPT指标。

Method: 在自由费米子BDI链中，于Rényi指数α=1/2处采用体态扣除的方法提取边界响应。使用了三种互补的技术手段：精确有限开链关系、大L极限下的Pfaffian求值、以及直接的正权重检验。

Result: 在质量反转（mass inversion）跨越时，边界响应趋近于|Δω|ln 2，其中Δω为绕数（winding number）的变化。该响应在原始体态形变下存活，在测试的局域边界扰动下保持，并能突破体态对偶（bulk duality）的破坏。

Conclusion: 对于所研究的家族而言，该边界响应可直接计数改变费米子SPT指标的Majorana通道数，提供了一种将stabilizer Rényi熵与拓扑不变量建立定量联系的方案。

Abstract: Open boundaries host symmetry-protected Majorana modes, yet their imprint on the stabilizer Rényi entropy is obscured by a nonuniversal volume law. At Rényi index $α=1/2$, we isolate a bulk-subtracted boundary response in free-fermion BDI chains using exact finite-open-chain relations, large-$L$ Pfaffian evaluations, and direct positive-weight checks. Across a mass inversion, this response approaches $|Δω|\ln 2$, where $Δω$ is the change in winding number. The response survives primitive bulk deformations, the tested local boundary perturbations, and broken bulk duality. Thus, for the families studied here, the boundary response counts the Majorana channels that change the fermionic SPT index.

</details>


### [96] [Security of quantum key distribution with passive basis choice and detection-efficiency mismatch for a realistic satellite setup](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09793)
*Egor Ivchenko,Aleksandr Khmelev,Vladimir Kurochkin,Anton Trushechkin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对BB84协议被动基矢选择中探测器效率不匹配这一实际问题，提出了一种更精确的密钥率估计方法，该方法同时考虑了四个阈值探测器间的效率不匹配以及诱骗态方法的适配，并通过Micius卫星与Zvenigorod地面站之间的QKD实验进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的被动基矢选择安全证明仅在探测器效率不匹配较小时才能获得非零密钥率，而实际系统中效率不匹配可能非常显著。因此需要一种更精确的密钥率估计方法来处理实际系统中显著的探测器效率不匹配问题。

Method: 提出了一种更精确的BB84协议被动基矢选择下的密钥率估计方法，该方法同时考虑了四个阈值探测器之间的检测效率不匹配，并适配了诱骗态方法。

Result: 该方法被用于估计Micius卫星与Zvenigorod地面站之间QKD实验的密钥率。

Conclusion: 通过考虑显著的探测器效率不匹配并适配诱骗态方法，本文提出的方法能在实际系统中给出更精确的密钥率估计，解决了现有安全证明在效率不匹配较大时密钥率为零的问题。

Abstract: Detection-efficiency mismatch is a common problem in realistic quantum key distribution (QKD) systems. The existing security proofs for the case of the passive basis choice provide a nonzero secret key rate only for a small detection-efficiency mismatch. Unfortunately, in realistic setups, the detection-efficiency mismatch can be significant. Here we present a more precise estimation of the secret key rate for the BB84 protocol with the passive basis choice, which accounts for the detection-efficiency mismatch between four threshold detectors as well as an adaptation of the decoy-state method. The suggested approach is used to estimate the secret key rate in a QKD experiment between the Micius satellite and the Zvenigorod ground station.

</details>


### [97] [From Barren Plateaus to SPSA Optimization in Variational Quantum Eigensolvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09810)
*Zhen Qin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次从理论层面系统刻画了贫瘠高原（BP）现象对有限测量下SPSA优化算法的影响，证明BP导致梯度能量指数衰减，进而使迭代次数和总测量预算呈指数增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究虽已从几何角度广泛探讨了BP现象的成因，但BP对实际优化算法在有限测量条件下的动态过程、迭代复杂度和测量预算的影响仍缺乏理论理解。

Method: 建立理论框架，推导SPSA梯度估计器的非渐近偏差与方差刻画，引入信噪比（SNR）分析量化梯度可靠性，并在有限测量条件下建立SPSA的收敛保证。

Result: 证明了与BP相关的梯度能量指数衰减会导致为达到固定相对优化精度所需的迭代次数呈指数增长，进而导致总测量预算呈指数增长。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了BP不仅是一个几何/可训练性问题，还会从根本上影响有限测量下VQE优化算法的实际计算复杂度，为设计缓解BP影响的策略提供了理论依据。

Abstract: The barren plateau (BP) phenomenon poses a fundamental challenge to the trainability of variational quantum eigensolvers (VQEs) by causing exponentially vanishing gradients as the system size increases. While extensive studies have investigated the geometric origins of BP, its impact on the optimization dynamics and complexity of practical algorithms under finite-shot measurements remains poorly understood. In this paper, we develop a theoretical framework that characterizes how the BP affects the optimization dynamics of the Simultaneous Perturbation Stochastic Approximation (SPSA) algorithm and quantifies the resulting iteration complexity and measurement budget. We derive non-asymptotic bias and variance characterizations of the SPSA gradient estimator, introduce a signal-to-noise ratio analysis to quantify gradient reliability, and establish convergence guarantees for SPSA under finite-shot measurements. Our results show that the exponentially decaying gradient energy associated with BP leads to an exponential increase in the number of iterations required to achieve a fixed relative optimization accuracy, which in turn results in an exponential increase in the total measurement budget.

</details>


### [98] [Quantum Information Flow under String-Diagram Rewriting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09823)
*Yi-Yu Lin,Chen-Ye Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新审视Coecke早期提出的"量子信息流"概念，并给出显式的弦图（string diagram）形式化描述。该工作区分了依赖当前图形表示的"表观通路径"与可通过连续重写兼容继承到终端解耦裸线因子的"真正通路径"，并据此正式定义了"Coecke流线"。文章以ZX演算为例展示该形式化，并在量子协议物理背景下将Coecke流解释为协议弦图中目标裸线态射因子的受约束准局域线状呈现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Coecke早期提出的"量子信息流"概念缺乏正式的弦图化形式化定义，本文旨在填补这一空白，为量子协议中的信息流动提供严格的语法层面的刻画。

Method: 给定一个语义保持的弦图重写序列，首先区分表观通路径（依赖当前图形呈现）与真正通路径（可通过兼容继承经连续重写到达终端解耦裸线因子），然后基于这种兼容继承关系正式定义Coecke流线，并以ZX演算作为弦图重写系统加以例证。

Result: 正式定义了Coecke流线概念，建立了其在弦图重写框架下的严格形式化，并在ZX演算中进行了具体演示。

Conclusion: Coecke流可被解释为量子协议弦图中目标裸线态射因子的受约束、准局域、线状呈现方式，为量子信息流提供了一个精确的范畴论/弦图级别的数学形式化框架。

Abstract: We revisit the notion of ``quantum information flow'' introduced in Bob Coecke's early work and seek to give it an explicit string-diagrammatic formalization. Given a semantics preserving string-diagram rewriting sequence, we first distinguish apparent through-paths, which depend on the current graphical presentation, from genuine through paths, which can be compatibly inherited through successive rewrites to a terminal decoupled bare wire factor. We then formally define a ``Coecke flow line'' in terms of this compatible inheritance relation.
  In particular, we use the ZX calculus, i.e., the ZX string diagram rewriting system, to illustrate the resulting formalism. In the physical setting of quantum protocols, a Coecke flow can be interpreted as a constrained, quasi-local, line-like presentation of a target bare wire morphism factor within the protocol string diagram.

</details>


### [99] [An upper bound for the purity of absolutely positive partial transpose states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09832)
*Anh T. Tran*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出了APPT双粒子态纯度的一个上界，该上界同时也是绝对可分态纯度的上界；对于qubit-qudit系统，这个上界恰好就是APPT（和绝对可分）态的最大纯度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态的分类中，绝对可分态是绝对正偏转置（APPT）态的子集，且二者在qubit-qudit系统中等价。此前对这类态的纯度上界缺乏系统研究，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 利用APPT态在任意全局酉变换下保持PPT性质的结构约束，通过数学推导给出APPT双粒子态纯度的上界表达式，并在qubit-qudit特殊情形下证明该上界可达到。

Result: 得到了APPT双粒子态纯度的上界（因此也是绝对可分态纯度的上界）；在qubit-qudit系统中，该上界与APPT态的最大纯度精确一致。

Conclusion: 该上界为APPT和绝对可分态的结构提供了新的定量刻画，特别在qubit-qudit系统中给出了最优的纯度约束。

Abstract: A quantum state is called absolutely separable (resp. absolutely positive partial transpose (APPT)) if it remains separable (resp. positive partial transpose (PPT)) under any global unitary transformation. It is known that the set of all absolutely separable states is a subset of the set of all APPT states. Moreover, these two sets are identical for qubit-qudit systems. In this note, we give an upper bound for the purity of APPT bipartite states (and therefore for the purity of absolutely separable bipartite states). For qubit-qudit systems, this upper bound becomes the maximum purity of APPT (and absolutely separable) states.

</details>


### [100] [Tensor network methods for non-perturbative dynamics of open quantum systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09850)
*Thibaut Lacroix,Adam Burgess,Nicola Lorenzoni,Julian Wiercinski,Kian Damezin,James Lim,Dario Tamascelli,Alex W. Chin,Moritz Cygorek,Brendon W. Lovett,Jonathan Keeling,Susana F. Huelga,Martin B. Plenio,Erik M. Gauger*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了利用张量网络方法处理开放量子系统动力学的非微扰、数值精确方法，克服了传统微扰方法（如马尔可夫主方程）在记忆核指数级标度上的计算瓶颈，并系统比较了各类方法的异同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放量子系统动力学超越微扰处理（通常与马尔可夫主方程相关）是一个计算挑战性极高的任务，因为记忆核存在不利的指数级标度。传统方法难以处理这种复杂度，因此需要新的框架来克服计算瓶颈。

Method: 该综述整合了近几十年来在量子信息和凝聚态物理背景下发展起来的张量网络方法，这些方法提供了一种新的形式和工具箱，能够构建非微扰、数值精确的开放量子系统动力学描述方法，并对不同方法之间的共性和差异进行系统比较。

Result: 文中展示张量网络框架使得开放量子系统动力学的非微扰、数值精确模拟达到可控的数值精度成为可能，综述旨在通过对比各类张量网络方法，对领域形成全面的视角。

Conclusion: 张量网络方法成功克服了开放量子系统在非微扰动力学模拟中的计算瓶颈，为领域提供了统一的、可达到可控精度的数值精确求解框架。

Abstract: The description of open quantum system dynamics beyond the perturbative treatment (usually associated with Markovian master equations) is a computationally challenging task due to the unfavorable exponential scaling of memory kernels. Developed over recent decades in the context of quantum information and condensed matter, tensor networks provide both a new formalism and a toolbox for overcoming previous computational bottlenecks. This framework enables the formulation of non-perturbative, numerically exact methods for describing the dynamics of open quantum systems to controllable numerical accuracy. In this review, we present these methods and discuss their commonalities and differences to paint a comprehensive view of the field.

</details>


### [101] [From Nonlinear Stochastic Differential Equations to Quantum Channels: The Kolmogorov--Lindblad Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09903)
*Hsuan-Cheng Wu,Xiantao Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出Kolmogorov–Lindblad映射（KLM），将非线性随机微分方程（SDE）的概率律精确编码为量子Lindblad方程，使经典SDE与开放量子动力学在量子计算框架下统一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性SDE在分子建模、药物发现、量化金融、随机学习和不确定性量化等领域至关重要，但其非线性漂移和对噪声实现的平均阻碍了直接的量子表示，需要一种数学上受控的量子映射方案。

Method: KLM在概率律层面进行精确映射，将密度算子编码为迹为一的量子密度算子。对每个布朗路径，路径密度可分解为半密度，其演化满足随机薛定谔方程；对纯态求平均得到Lindblad方程，其对角核恰为Fokker–Planck密度，经典扩散由厄米跳变算子引起的退相干表示。采用保持结构的Galerkin投影在有限维下保持Lindblad形式。

Result: 在双阱Langevin动力学和含噪Lorenz–63系统的数值实验中，统计可观测量快速收敛。经典双时关联函数具有精确的量子回归公式。

Conclusion: KLM为从一般非线性随机动力学到量子通道提供了数学受控的路径，将函数逼近和相干算子访问确定为决定算法效率的剩余因素，为经典SDE提供了量子原生计算基础。

Abstract: Nonlinear stochastic differential equations (SDEs) underlie molecular modeling and drug discovery, quantitative finance, stochastic learning, and uncertainty quantification. Their expectations, event probabilities, and time correlations are therefore natural targets for quantum computation, but nonlinear drift and averaging over noise realizations obstruct a direct quantum representation. We develop an exact \emph{Kolmogorov--Lindblad mapping} (KLM) at the level of the probability law, encoded natively as a trace-one quantum density operator. For each Brownian realization, the pathwise density admits a half-density whose evolution is a stochastic Schrödinger equation. Averaging the associated pure states yields a Lindblad equation for $Γ(t)$ whose diagonal kernel is exactly the Fokker--Planck density, $p(t,x)=Γ(t;x,x)$, with classical diffusion represented by decoherence through Hermitian jump operators. Statistical observables become quantum expectations without the unknown time-dependent normalization introduced by direct amplitude encoding of the density, and classical two-time correlations admit an exact quantum regression formula. A structure-preserving Galerkin projection retains the Lindblad form at finite dimension, placing classical SDEs and open quantum dynamics on the same quantum-native computational footing. Numerical experiments for double-well Langevin dynamics and noisy Lorenz--63 exhibit rapid convergence of statistical observables. KLM thus provides a mathematically controlled path from general nonlinear stochastic dynamics to quantum channels, while isolating function approximation and coherent operator access as the remaining determinants of algorithmic efficiency.

</details>


### [102] [Surface passivation for narrowing optical linewidth of silicon T centers in nanophotonic devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09904)
*Fariba Islam,Chang-Min Lee,Kyu-Young Kim,Sorah Fischer,Purbita Purkayastha,Edo Waks*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文报道了利用原子层沉积Al2O3钝化硅表面，系统地缩小了硅T中心的光学线宽（最大减少57%），并通过带隙以上光照和光谱烧孔测量，将均匀线宽上限定为约75 MHz，为可扩展量子光子学应用提供了CMOS兼容的路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硅T中心是电信波段兼容的可扩展量子信息技术中有前景的自旋-光子界面，但其在纳米光子学中的主要挑战是光谱扩散——表面和体电荷态的局部电场波动会展宽光学跃迁并降低光子不可区分性。因此，直接抑制光谱扩散的策略对于改进基于T中心的量子光子器件至关重要。

Method: 采用原子层沉积（ALD）Al2O3钝化硅表面，系统测量钝化前后T中心光学线宽的变化；利用互补的带隙以上光照和光谱烧孔（spectral hole burning）测量来区分均匀线宽与光谱扩散成分，并确定均匀线宽的上限。

Result: Al2O3钝化使T中心发射线宽最多减少57%；带隙以上光照和光谱烧孔实验表明剩余线宽中含有由邻近电荷陷阱引起的光谱扩散成分，同时将均匀线宽上限定为约75 MHz。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一条CMOS兼容的路径，可通过表面钝化有效抑制光谱扩散，从而从硅T中心产生不可区分光子，支撑可扩展的量子光子学应用。

Abstract: Silicon T centers are promising spin-photon interfaces in solid-state platforms for telecom-compatible, scalable quantum information technologies. A major challenge for T centers in nanophotonics is spectral diffusion, where fluctuations in the local electric-field environment from surface and bulk charge states broaden the optical transition and reduce photon indistinguishability. Strategies that directly suppress spectral diffusion are therefore critical for improving T-center-based quantum photonic devices. Here, we use atomic-layer-deposited Al2O3 to passivate the silicon surface and demonstrate a systematic narrowing of T center optical linewidths. Across our measurements, Al2O3 passivation reduces the T center emission linewidth by up to 57%. Complementary above-bandgap illumination and spectral hole burning measurements show that the remaining linewidth contains a significant spectral-diffusion component caused by adjacent charge traps, while placing an upper bound of approximately 75 MHz on the homogeneous linewidth. This work provides a CMOS-compatible path toward generating indistinguishable photons from silicon T centers for scalable quantum photonic applications.

</details>


### [103] [Learning Clifford-structured quantum unitaries and Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09912)
*Arkopal Dutt,Dale Jacobs,John Jeang,Saeed Mehraban,Vladimir Podolskii*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在Pauli基下稠密但具有简洁Clifford分解的n量子比特酉算子U和哈密顿量H的学习算法。作者引入了一种针对Clifford酉算子的不可知（agnostic）层析成像协议，并利用该协议得到了对Clifford extent有界的一类酉算子和哈密顿量的层析成像协议，将哈密顿量的可学习性从Pauli稀疏情形推广到Pauli稠密但Clifford结构化的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于结构化量子酉算子和哈密顿量的学习算法主要针对在Pauli基下局部或稀疏的过程。然而许多实际感兴趣的量子系统在Pauli基下是稠密的（稀疏度可达Ω(2^n)），因此需要探索除Pauli稀疏性之外的其他可学习结构，特别是Clifford可分解结构。

Method: 作者首先引入了一个针对Clifford酉算子的不可知层析成像协议：给定未知酉算子U的查询访问，且U的最优Clifford保真度为opt，该协议能输出一个保真度≥opt−ε的Clifford酉算子，运行时间为poly(n, (1/ε)^{log(1/ε)})。随后将该协议应用于具有有界Clifford extent（即U=Σ_i α_i C_i且Σ_i|α_i|有界）的酉算子和哈密顿量的层析成像。

Result: 获得了针对Clifford extent有界的酉算子和哈密顿量的层析成像协议，将哈密顿量学习的适用范围从Pauli稀疏分解扩展到Pauli基下稠密（稀疏度Ω(2^n)）但具有Clifford结构的过程。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了Clifford结构作为除Pauli稀疏性之外另一种可学习的量子结构表征，拓展了量子哈密顿量和酉算子学习的理论边界。

Abstract: Learning algorithms for structured quantum unitaries and Hamiltonians have primarily considered classes of processes that are local or sparse in the Pauli basis. We turn our attention to learning $n$-qubit quantum unitaries $U$ and Hamiltonians $H$, given query access to $U$ or the unitary evolution of $H$, that may be dense in the Pauli basis but still admit concise Clifford decompositions. Specifically, we consider unitaries (or Hamiltonians) of the form $U = \sum_i α_i C_i$ over Cliffords $C_i$ with bounded Clifford extent $\sum_i |α_i|$. To extract this Clifford structure, we introduce an agnostic tomography protocol for Clifford unitaries that given query access to an unknown unitary $U$ with optimal Clifford fidelity $\textsf{opt}$, outputs a Clifford unitary witnessing fidelity $\geq \textsf{opt} - \varepsilon$ for some error $\varepsilon > 0$, in time $\textsf{poly}(n,(1/\varepsilon)^{\log(1/\varepsilon)})$. We then apply this protocol to obtain tomography protocols for unitaries and Hamiltonians that have bounded Clifford extent. This extends learnability of Hamiltonians from those with sparse Pauli decompositions to those that are dense (i.e., has sparsity $Ω(2^n)$) in the Pauli basis but are Clifford structured.

</details>


### [104] [From Koszul-Complex Stabilizer Models to Superselection Profiles: Topological Rigidity and Nonsplit Extensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09913)
*Hao Song*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出Koszul-复形稳定子模型统一框架，引入拓扑超选择剖面S_σ来研究平移对称性富集的拓扑相。通过矩阵级Schanuel引理方法，在有限分辨假设下证明了拓扑CSS码的平移SET序刚性，并展示了多层间粘合携带对单层不可见的SET数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将Koszul-复形稳定子模型统一了环面码层次结构与双变量自行车码，但缺乏系统研究这些模型中平移对称性富集的拓扑（SET）相的通用同调框架。作者希望建立一种统一的数学工具来分析平移不变的CSS码的拓扑激发的超选择结构及其SET序。

Method: 引入拓扑超选择剖面S_σ:=τ≥1 R Hom_R(cokerφ_σ的补,R)，其同调层E_σ^ℓ编码扇区、融合与平移作用。对规则Koszul模型显式计算E_σ^ℓ，使用矩阵级Schanuel引理证明刚性定理，并通过有限分辨假设下唯一确定平移SET序。利用Tor计算有限尺寸Z逻辑。

Result: 规则Koszul模型得到单层S_σ剖面，最多一个正次数层E_σ^ℓ非零。在有限分辨假设下证明非零层与次数唯一决定拓扑CSS码的平移SET序；素qudit情形假设自动成立，有限层的平移核给出精确的最小粗粒化。构造了可容许的单层数据。在单层之外，3D qubit环面码的八个分裂/非分裂扩张模型共享相同E_σ^ℓ层但具有不同的尺寸依赖基态简并度，从而属于不同的平移SET相。

Conclusion: 层间粘合携带了对单个同调层不可见的SET数据，说明仅看各层的E_σ^ℓ不足以完全刻画平移SET相。所提出的超选择剖面框架为系统分类平移不变CSS码的SET序提供了统一而有力的工具。

Abstract: Koszul-complex stabilizer models unify the toric-code hierarchy and bivariate-bicycle codes in one homological framework. To study translation-symmetry-enriched topological (SET) phases in these models and, more generally, in translation-invariant Calderbank-Shor-Steane (CSS) codes with stabilizer maps $(\varphi_X,\varphi_Z)$, we introduce the associated topological superselection profile $\mathscr S_σ:=τ_{\geq1}\mathbf R\!\operatorname{Hom}_R(\overline{\operatorname{coker}\varphi_σ},R),\ σ=X,Z$. Its cohomology layers $E_σ^\ell:=H^\ell(\mathscr S_σ)\cong_R\operatorname{Ext}_R^\ell(\overline{\operatorname{coker}\varphi_σ},R)$ encode sectors, fusion, and translation action. When finite, the $\ell=1,2,\ldots$ layers describe pointlike, looplike, and higher-dimensional excitations; $\mathscr S_σ$ retains inter-layer gluing. For regular Koszul models, we compute $E_σ^\ell$ explicitly, find $\mathscr S_σ$ single-layer, and obtain finite-size $Z$-logicals from $\operatorname{Tor}$. Single-layer means that at most one positive-degree layer $E_σ^\ell$ is nonzero. Our matrix-level Schanuel-lemma method proves rigidity: under a finite-resolution hypothesis, the nonzero layer and degree uniquely determine a topological CSS code's translation SET order at the stable translation-invariant Clifford level. For prime qudits the hypothesis is automatic, and a finite layer's translation kernel gives the exact minimal coarse graining to a toric-code stack. We realize admissible single-layer data. Beyond this regime, split and nonsplit extensions of 3D qubit toric codes yield eight models sharing all identical $E_σ^\ell$ layers with trivial translation action but having distinct size-dependent ground-state degeneracies, hence distinct translation SET phases. Thus inter-layer gluing carries SET data invisible to individual layers.

</details>


### [105] [Decoupling 2D translation-invariant topological CSS codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09915)
*Yifei Wang,Zhongyi Ni,Mingxin He,Jinguo Liu,Yingfei Gu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明二维平移不变的拓扑CSS码在取其最大任意子保持超晶格后，必然可通过局部幺正变换解耦为环面码和乘积态的堆叠，并提供了高效算法及超胞尺寸和算子扩散的界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知二维平移不变拓扑CSS码在粗粒化后局部等价于环面码的堆叠，但现有构造通常会破坏超过去除任意子置换平移所需的平移对称性。这留下了一个开放问题：是否存在进一步的阻碍，使得某些码在保持更充分的平移对称性时无法解耦为环面码堆叠。本文旨在证明不存在这样的阻碍。

Method: 通过考虑最大任意子保持超晶格（maximal anyon-preserving superlattice），证明每个此类码都存在局部幺正变换将其解耦为环面码与乘积态；同时给出显式构造解耦映射的高效算法，并推导所需超胞尺寸和算子扩散的界；该解耦在一般情况下不需要额外的辅助比特，并可推广到具有合适边界条件的有限系统。

Result: 证明了在最大任意子保持超晶格上，所有二维平移不变拓扑CSS码均可通过局部幺正变换解耦为环面码和乘积态，即不存在进一步的结构障碍；提供了高效的显式构造算法以及超胞尺寸和算子扩散的定量界。

Conclusion: 此前仅知这些码局部等价于环面码堆叠，但对称性保持程度不确定；本文消除这一不确定性，确认最大任意子保持超晶格足以实现完全解耦，且构造可高效完成。

Abstract: Two-dimensional translation-invariant topological CSS codes on qubits are known to be locally equivalent, after coarse-graining, to stacks of toric codes. However, existing constructions generally break more translation symmetry than is required to remove anyon-permuting translations, leaving open whether any further obstruction exists. We prove that no such obstruction occurs: after passing to the maximal anyon-preserving superlattice, every such code admits a local unitary decoupling into toric codes and product states. We further provide an efficient algorithm for explicitly constructing the decoupling map, together with bounds on the required supercell size and operator spreading. The decoupling requires no additional ancillas in generic cases and extends to finite systems with suitable boundary conditions.

</details>


### [106] [A Quantum Coherence Microscope in the Hubbard Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09924)
*Lin Su,Michal Szurek,Alec Douglas,Ceren B. Dag,Markus Greiner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种基于Talbot效应的量子相干显微镜，能够将离对角关联映射为具有近单格点分辨率的密度信号，从而实现对超流-莫特转变的局域探测和在工程势阱中超越最近邻的相干性测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子气体显微镜虽能以单原子分辨率提供多体态的投影快照，但在空间分辨上测量离对角关联（非对角关联）长期以来仍是一项挑战。作者希望弥合这一空白，实现对量子相干性的空间分辨测量。

Method: 利用Talbot效应，设计了一种量子相干显微镜，通过受控的Talbot演化将离对角关联映射为格点分辨的密度信号，从而实现近单格点分辨率的相干性测量。该技术被应用于三维光晶格单层的超流-莫特转变局域探测，以及工程势阱中超越最近邻的相干性测量。

Result: 成功实现了离对角关联的空间分辨测量，并以此局域探测了超流-莫特转变过程，同时能够在工程势阱中测量超越最近邻的相干性。

Conclusion: 通过受控物质波演化与再捕获，将离对角关联映射到密度信号上，为访问超越密度基的观测量开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Quantum coherence underlies collective quantum phenomena and emerging quantum technologies. Quantum gas microscopes have transformed quantum simulation by providing projective snapshots of many-body states with single-atom resolution, but spatially resolved measurements of off-diagonal correlations have remained elusive. Here, using the Talbot effect, we introduce a quantum coherence microscope that maps off-diagonal correlations onto site-resolved density signals with near-single-site resolution. We use this technique to locally probe the superfluid-Mott transition in a layer of a three-dimensional optical lattice and to measure coherence beyond nearest neighbors in an engineered potential landscape. By mapping off-diagonal correlations onto density signals through controlled Talbot evolution, this work opens new possibilities for accessing observables beyond the density basis through tailored matter-wave evolution and recapture.

</details>

### [107] [Optimization of the Repumping Parameters for a Sodium Laser Guide Star Magnetometer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08983)
*Yucheng Yang,Chunyang Lei,Kai Guo,Shuai Wang*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了钠激光导星作为中层大气磁强计的工作模式，其以本地拉莫尔频率调制589nm激光，并将部分功率分流至再泵浦光以恢复暗基态中的原子。针对最强驱动的速度群进行偏振读出，需要最优通量分数的2.8倍，而结合两个可观测量的散粒噪声品质因数需要2倍，这超出了商业导星激光器所能提供的范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钠激光导星通常用于天文自适应光学，但本文探索其作为中层大气（中气层）磁强计的新用途，利用钠原子对磁场敏感的能级分裂来测量中层大气的磁场信息，需要解决激光功率分配和偏振读出优化的问题。

Method: 以本地拉莫尔频率调制589nm激光，将部分激光功率分流到再泵浦光以恢复因进入暗基态而损失的原子；针对最强驱动的速度群进行偏振读出，并构建结合两个可观测量的散粒噪声品质因数进行优化分析。

Result: 分析表明，偏振读出需要最优通量分数的2.8倍功率，而散粒噪声品质因数需要2倍的功率，这两项需求均超出当前商业导星激光器所能提供的范围。

Conclusion: 现有的商业钠导星激光器无法满足将钠激光导星用作中层大气磁强计所需的功率和通量分配要求，需要更高功率或改进的激光系统才能实现这一应用。

Abstract: A sodium laser guide star operated as a mesospheric magnetometer modulates a 589 nm laser at the local Larmor frequency and usually diverts a fraction of its power to a repumping light that recovers atoms lost to the dark ground state.The polarization, read out for the most strongly driven velocity group, calls for 2.8 times the flux optimal fraction, and a shot noise figure of merit combining the two observables for 2 times, beyond the range commercial guide star lasers provide.

</details>

### [108] [Fluid-Structure Interaction and Underwater Hydrostatic Implosion of Thin-Walled Metallic Cylinders in Semi-Confined Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07647)
*Bolaji Oladipo,Helio Matos,Arun Shukla,Sumanta Das*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本研究利用LS-DYNA中的结构化ALE公式，对半约束流体环境中金属圆柱体的静水压溃动态行为及流固耦合进行了全面数值研究。模型精确复现了3.69 MPa的实验压溃压力，并预测水锤峰值误差仅1.01%。研究发现钛合金比铝合金产生更剧烈的压溃、更高的水锤压力（超70 MPa）及更大的能量积累；低长径比导致更突然的压溃，而大约束直径强化射流和流体速度。全流场FSI分析捕捉到了径向射流、峰值流速和内部空化等仅凭压力-时间曲线无法观测的关键现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下结构（如深海压力容器、海底管道）在极端静水载荷下可能发生压溃，其动态行为与周围流体存在强烈的流固耦合效应，而传统方法仅依赖压力-时间历程无法全面揭示压溃过程中的关键物理现象。因此需要系统研究材料类型、长径比和约束直径等参数对压溃行为及能量传递的影响机制，为水下结构的设计与安全评估提供定量指导。

Method: 采用LS-DYNA中的结构化任意拉格朗日-欧拉（ALE）公式对半约束流体环境中金属圆柱体的静水压溃进行数值模拟。首先通过实验结果（压溃压力3.69 MPa）验证模型精度，随后系统变化材料类型（铝、钛）、圆柱体长径比（L/D）和约束直径三个参数，分析压溃行为、压力演化及流体运动的耦合响应。

Result: 模型预测压溃压力与实验吻合，水锤峰值误差仅1.01%。钛合金圆柱体表现出更尖锐的压溃、超70 MPa的水锤压力以及更高的动能和应变能积累。低L/D比导致更突然的压溃，而高L/D比促进渐进对称变形。更大的约束直径加剧射流形成并提高流体速度。全流场FSI分析揭示了径向射流、峰值流体速度和内部空化等压力-时间曲线无法体现的现象。

Conclusion: 几何参数、材料刚度和约束环境共同主导压溃诱导的能量传递。全流场FSI分析能够捕获压力-时间历史无法揭示的关键物理现象（径向射流、峰值流速、内部空化），为深海压力壳体、海底管道等水下结构在极端静水载荷下的设计和安全评估提供了定量的工程指导。

Abstract: This study presents a comprehensive numerical investigation of the dynamic behavior and fluid-structure interactions (FSI) of metallic cylinders undergoing hydrostatic collapse in semi-confined fluid environments using a structured Arbitrary Eulerian-Lagrangian (ALE) formulation in LS-DYNA. The numerical model reproduces the experimentally measured collapse pressure of 3.69 MPa and predicts the first water hammer peak with a 1.01% error, demonstrating high predictive fidelity. Following validation, the effects of material type (aluminum and titanium), cylinder slenderness ratio (L/D), and confinement diameter on collapse behavior, pressure evolution, and fluid motion are examined. Titanium cylinders exhibited sharper collapses, higher water hammer pressures exceeding 70 MPa, and greater kinetic and strain energy accumulation than aluminum due to their higher stiffness and yield strength. Lower L/D ratios produced more abrupt collapses, whereas higher L/D ratios promoted more gradual, axisymmetric deformation. Larger confinement diameters intensified jet formation and increased fluid velocities. The simulations provide mechanistic insight into the coupling between structural deformation and surrounding fluid, showing that geometry, material stiffness, and confinement govern collapse-induced energy transfer. Full-field FSI analysis captures key phenomena, including radial jetting, peak fluid velocities, and internal cavitation, that are not evident from pressure-time histories alone. These findings provide quantitative guidance for the design and safety assessment of subsea pressure housings, marine pipelines, and other underwater structures subjected to extreme hydrostatic loading.

</details>


### [109] [Set-up and Characterisation of Atmospheric Boundary Layers in the 10'x5' Wind Tunnel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07764)
*Sita M. Nair,Kevin Gouder*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本论文研究在风洞中利用Irwin尖塔（无地板粗糙元）复现海洋大气边界层（ABL），开发了两种剖面：一种作为国际风工程标准间的通用框架，另一种更适合模拟英吉利海峡和北海风电场来流。采用激光多普勒测速（LDA）和多孔探针（MHP）进行流场测量，验证了该方法的可靠性与经济性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大气边界层对暴露于大气环境的物体有重要影响，但因全尺寸实测成本高昂，需要在风洞中低成本、可靠地复现海洋ABL，以为海上风能系统优化提供依据。

Method: 使用Irwin尖塔（不含地板粗糙元）生成两种海洋ABL剖面；采用LDA和多孔探针（MHP）测量流场特性，对比两种测量技术的精度；通过归一化速度、湍流强度、展向均匀性及频谱分析评估所模拟剖面的质量。

Result: MHP与LDA对比显示：流向速度偏差小于1%，三个速度分量的标准差低于5%。所开发的剖面与归一化速度和湍流强度等目标指标良好吻合，展向均匀性和频谱分析确认了不同来流速度下模拟的稳健性。

Conclusion: Irwin尖塔是一种经济且可靠的海洋ABL模拟方法，为优化海上风能系统提供了有价值的参考。

Abstract: The Atmospheric Boundary Layer (ABL) plays a critical role in influencing objects exposed to atmospheric conditions, making its study crucial. Due to the high cost of real-world testing, this thesis focuses on replicating marine ABLs in a wind tunnel environment. Two profiles were developed: one that served as a framework for establishing commonality among the various international wind engineering standards ('Profile 1'), and a second profile, which is more suitable for modelling the inflow to wind farms in the English Channel and the North Sea ('Profile 2').
  The ABLs were generated using Irwin spires without floor roughness elements, and the flow characteristics were measured using Laser Doppler Anemometry (LDA) and a multi-hole probe (MHP). MHP showed a reasonably high accuracy when compared to LDA, with less than 1% deviation in the streamwise velocity and under 5% standard deviation in the streamwise, spanwise, and wall-normal velocity components. The profiles achieved good agreement with target metrics such as normalised velocity and turbulence intensity. Spanwise uniformity and spectral analysis confirmed the robustness of the simulation across varying inflow velocities.
  Overall, Irwin spires proved to be a cost-effective and reliable method for simulating marine ABLs, offering valuable insights for optimising offshore wind energy systems.

</details>


### [110] [Splashing velocity of a viscous liquid squeezed between two parallel disks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07960)
*Navin Kumar Chandra,Pierre Perrier,David Brutin*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个理论框架来预测两个平行圆盘之间被挤压粘性液体的喷射速度，通过完整考虑液体惯性（局部惯性和对流惯性）并引入动量平均喷射速度，解决了现有理论高估实验值的问题，在广泛参数范围内与实验取得了良好一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挤压薄膜流体力学虽被广泛研究，但溅射速度的定量预测仍悬而未决，现有理论显著高估实验结果，缺乏能够准确预测喷射速度的理论框架。

Method: 建立了两平行圆盘间被挤压粘性液体喷射速度的理论框架。关键改进在于：(1) 完整处理液体惯性（同时考虑局部惯性和对流惯性），弥补了先前理论对惯性处理不完整的缺陷；(2) 引入动量平均喷射速度替代峰值喷射速度来表征溅射速度。

Result: 通过在广泛参数范围内与实验对比，该理论框架获得了良好的一致性，解决了现有理论明显高估实验结果的问题。

Conclusion: 理论与实验的偏差源于两方面：液体惯性处理不完整，以及用峰值喷射速度代表溅射速度不当。通过完善惯性项并采用动量平均喷射速度，实现了对挤压溅射速度的准确定量预测。

Abstract: Squeezing of a liquid film between two approaching solid surfaces can generate a high-speed peripheral splash. Despite extensive studies on squeeze-film hydrodynamics, quantitative prediction of the splash velocity remains unresolved, with existing theory significantly overestimating experiments. Here, we present a theoretical framework to predict the ejection velocity of viscous liquid squeezed between two parallel circular disks. We show that discrepancy of theory from experiments arise due to incomplete treatment of liquid inertia and from using peak ejection velocity to represent splash velocity. By accounting for both local and convective inertia, and introducing a momentum-averaged ejection velocity, we obtain good agreement with experiments over a wide range of parameters.

</details>


### [111] [Finite basis physics-informed neural networks with hard constraints for viscous fluid flow in highly perforated domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08114)
*Jeeeun Lee,Denis Korolev,Miro Duhovic,Seong Su Kim*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出了将有限基物理信息神经网络（FBPINNs）与硬边界约束相结合的方法，用于求解高度穿孔域中的斯托克斯流动问题。该方法通过域分解和边界条件的精确编码，克服了传统PINN在大量穿孔情况下精度和效率下降的缺陷，实现了与穿孔数量弱相关的收敛性，并提供了并行化的高效神经框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 穿孔微结构引起的复杂边界条件和精细流动特征是标准神经网络难以解析的。传统PINNs即使结合先进训练技术，随着穿孔数量增加仍会出现精度和效率损失，主要源于通过罚项软性施加边界条件导致的刚度问题、梯度冲突和近边界流动结构分辨率差。硬约束虽可精确编码边界条件，但其全局近似性质会引入不期望的非局部效应。

Method: 采用基于域分解和局部化原理的FBPINNs，并结合硬边界约束来高效编码与穿孔相关的边界条件。硬约束将边界条件精确嵌入网络ansatz中，而FBPINNs的局部化机制缓解了硬约束的全局非局部效应。同时提供了理论论证，聚焦于FBPINNs的局部化与逼近性质。

Result: 该方法有助于缓解谱偏差，提升整体精度，并且收敛性受穿孔数量的影响较弱，从而提供了高效且高度可并行化的神经网络框架。理论分析进一步支持了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: FBPINNs结合硬边界约束为高度穿孔域中的斯托克斯流问题提供了一种高效、精确且可扩展的求解方案，兼具理论支撑与实用价值。

Abstract: In this work, viscous fluid flow governed by the Stokes equations in highly perforated domains is studied using physics-informed neural networks (PINNs). Perforated microstructures induce complex boundary conditions and fine-scale flow features that are difficult for standard neural networks to resolve. Conventional PINNs, even when combined with advanced training techniques, can suffer from a loss of accuracy and efficiency as the number of perforations increases. One important source of this difficulty is the soft enforcement of boundary conditions through penalty terms, which can lead to stiffness, gradient conflicts, and poor resolution of near-boundary flow structures. Hard constraints provide an alternative by encoding boundary conditions exactly into the network ansatz, but may introduce undesirable non-local effects due to the global nature of the approximation. To address these challenges, finite basis PINNs (FBPINNs), which are based on domain decomposition and localisation principles, are used together with hard boundary constraints that efficiently encode perforation-related boundary conditions. This approach helps mitigate spectral bias, improves overall accuracy, and exhibits convergence that is only weakly affected by the number of perforations, thereby providing an efficient and highly parallelisable neural network framework. The proposed approach is further supported with theoretical arguments, specifically focusing on the localisation and approximation properties of FBPINNs.

</details>


### [112] [Eikonal Regularisation in Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Three-Dimensional Level-Set Advection: Transferability of Two-Dimensional Design Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08322)
*Muhammad Akbar Khan*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文系统地研究了物理信息神经网络（PINN）在水平集界面平流问题中eikonal正则化权重的选择，通过4个三维基准（平移球、旋转球、开槽球、逆向涡流）、6个权重、3个随机种子、完整训练预算下的预注册选择规则，验证了最优权重随精确解偏离符号距离函数程度跨四个数量级（10⁻¹到10⁻⁵）转移的规律；发现小权重下种子间标准差等于误差本身，正则化可将变异性降低一个数量级以上；与五阶WENO格式相比，经典方法在所有基准上更精确；并指出相对L₂误差无法证明薄特征保持，提出特征受限度量来弥补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前二维研究识别出eikonal正则化权重是PINN水平集界面平流方法的主导超参数，且其最优值在刚体运动与变形流动间跨越四个数量级，但未验证这些结论是否推广到三维，也未检验单种子结果是否能经受随机种子间运行变异性的考验。

Method: 在4个三维基准（平移球、旋转球、开槽球、逆向涡流）上重复权重选择实验，采用三个随机种子、六个权重值、完整训练预算，并使用预注册的选择规则进行系统化超参数扫描；采用多种子协议量化运行间变异性；引入特征受限的相对误差度量评估薄特征保持能力，并以相同网格和误差度量与五阶WENO经典格式对标。

Result: 最优权重排序在三维中成功转移：所选权重跟踪精确解偏离符号距离函数的程度，跨四个数量级（从解恰好滿足该性质时的10⁻¹到界面被拉伸时的10⁻⁵）；数值转移仅在基准层面成立，4个基准中2个不变而2个不转移；小权重下种子间标准差等于误差本身，正则化将其降低一个数量级以上；五阶WENO在所有4个问题上更精确，平滑刚体平流上优两个数量级，但差距随几何难度增大而缩小，且在体积守恒上的差距小于场范数；相对L₂误差不能证明薄特征保持，提出了可用的特征受限度量。

Conclusion: 二维研究发现的三维扩展：最优权重排序规律可以从二维推广到三维，但具体数值需要逐基准验证而非直接继承；多种子协议表明eikonal正则化不仅能提升精度还能显著提升可复现性；与经典WENO格式相比PINN仍有精度差距，且误差度量选择需谨慎，相对L₂误差不适合用于薄特征保持的验证。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks applied to the level-set formulation of interface advection commonly augment the residual and initial-condition losses with an eikonal regulariser, penalising the deviation of $\|\nablaφ\|$ from unity. A previous two-dimensional study identified this weight as the dominant hyperparameter and found its optimum shifts by four orders of magnitude between rigid-body and deforming flows, but left open whether these principles transfer to three dimensions and whether single-seed results survive run-to-run variability. We answer both by repeating the weight selection across four 3D benchmarks (translating sphere, rotating sphere, slotted sphere, reversed vortex), sweeping six weights with three seeds at full training budget under a pre-registered selection rule. The ordering transfers: the selected weight tracks how far the exact solution departs from the signed-distance property, spanning four decades from $10^{-1}$ where it holds exactly to $10^{-5}$ where the interface is stretched. Values transfer only benchmark by benchmark; two of four carry over unchanged and two do not, so inheritance must be verified. The multi-seed protocol reveals that at small weights the seed-to-seed standard deviation equals the error itself, and the regulariser reduces it by more than an order of magnitude, buying reproducibility as well as accuracy. We benchmark against a fifth-order WENO solver on identical grids and error measures; the classical scheme is more accurate on all four problems, by two orders of magnitude on smooth rigid advection, with a margin that narrows with geometric difficulty and is smaller in volume conservation than in the field norm. Finally, we show that the relative $L_2$ error cannot certify the preservation of thin features, and report a feature-restricted measure that can.

</details>


### [113] [Correlated collisions and history filtering: unraveling and reproducing the statistics of coalescing particles in turbulence from the ghost-particle framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08379)
*Fanxi Gong,Ewe-Wei Saw*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过三类DNS模拟（无相互作用幽灵粒子、碰撞-聚并-再补给粒子和无补给聚并粒子）系统研究了湍流中聚并粒子与无碰撞幽灵粒子的统计关系，提出历史过滤框架（碰撞关联时间τ_cc）统一了两类系统的碰撞核与径向分布函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解湍流中聚并粒子的统计特性及其与无碰撞幽灵粒子的联系，是气溶胶/云物理等领域的关键问题。幽灵粒子系统常被用作近似工具，但其与真实聚并系统的偏差来源尚不清楚，需要系统性厘清。

Method: 在相同流动条件下执行三类直接数值模拟（DNS）：(1)无相互作用的幽灵粒子；(2)碰撞即聚合并随机位置补充单体（CR）；(3)碰撞聚并但不补充（CN）。分析St=0.01-3.0范围的单分散单体，引入速度过滤RDF g_G^(-)(r)、历史过滤幽灵核、碰撞关联时间τ_cc等诊断量，并考察重复碰撞随St的指数衰减。

Result: 幽灵粒子系统的碰撞核K和接触RDF g(r)在全部St范围内均高于聚并系统。速度过滤RDF g_G^(-)(d)能合理估计CR接触RDF，残余偏差源于幽灵粒子系统中连续碰撞之间的关联。历史过滤幽灵粒子核可复现聚并系统的碰撞核。τ_cc在研究的St范围内具有有限且狭窄的取值区间。重复碰撞分数随St指数衰减。

Conclusion: 通过历史过滤框架，聚并粒子系统与幽灵粒子系统得到统一：将幽灵粒子系统中在τ_cc时间窗口内有碰撞历史的粒子滤除后，其RDF与碰撞核均与聚并系统一致。这为利用幽灵粒子模拟预测聚并统计提供了定量修正机制。

Abstract: This is the first in a series of papers aimed at understanding the statistics of coalescing particles in turbulent flow and their relation to collisionless ghost particles. We perform three families of Direct Numerical Simulations (DNS) under identical flow conditions: ghost particles without mutual interactions; particles that coalesce upon collision with lost monomers replenished at random positions (CR); and particles with the same collision-coalescence kinetics without replenishment (CN). All analyses are monodisperse and concern only monomers. Across $St=0.01\text{-}3.0$, the ghost-particle system has higher collision kernels ($K$) and radial distribution functions (RDFs, $g(r)$) near contact than the coalescing systems. A velocity-filtered RDF, $g_{\mathrm{G}}^{(-)}(r)$, provides a reasonable estimate of the CR contact RDF, $g_{\mathrm{CR}}(d)$. We show that the residual discrepancy between $g_{\mathrm{G}}^{(-)}(d)$ and $g_{\mathrm{CR}}(d)$, and between the corresponding kernels, arises from correlations among successive collisions in the ghost-particle system. A history-filtered ghost-particle kernel, excluding such correlations, reproduces the coalescing-system kernel. We introduce a collision-correlation time $τ_{\mathrm{cc}}$ that quantifies how long current collisions influence future events and find that it has a finite, narrow range across the studied $St$. Filtering particles with collisions within a period comparable to $τ_{\mathrm{cc}}$ yields a history-filtered RDF that reproduces those of both coalescing systems. Finally, the fraction of repeated collisions involving identical particles decays exponentially with $St$. These results unify the coalescing and ghost-particle systems as a history-filtered framework.

</details>


### [114] [K41 Scaling in Bubble-Induced Turbulence Arises from Single-Bubble Wakes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08502)
*Dabao Li,Zhilong Jin,Guangzhao Zhou*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过高分辨率直接数值模拟（DNS）研究气泡诱导湍流（BIT）中Kolmogorov（K41）标度的来源，发现2/3标度仅出现在气泡尾流区域，且K41标度源于单个气泡尾流的叠加，并据此推导出稀薄BIT中耗散率的标度律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究气泡诱导湍流中K41标度（结构函数2/3标度）的物理来源，理解其是否真正符合经典湍流理论，并建立与实验结果一致的耗散率标度关系。

Method: 采用界面分辨的高分辨率直接数值模拟（DNS），按区域划分计算速度结构函数；与匹配参数的单气泡DNS进行对比分析；基于尾流叠加机制推导稀薄BIT的耗散率标度律。

Result: 2/3标度仅存在于气泡尾流区域内；全BIT场的K41标度是由单个气泡尾流的叠加所致；推导的耗散率标度与文献中的实验结果一致。

Conclusion: 气泡诱导湍流中的K41标度并非来自经典的湍流级联过程，而是源于各气泡独立尾流的空间叠加效应；所提出的耗散率标度律能够正确描述稀薄BIT的物理行为。

Abstract: We use high-resolution, interface-resolved direct numerical simulations (DNS) to investigate the origin of Kolmogorov (K41) scaling in bubble-induced turbulence (BIT). Region-wise velocity structure functions show that the 2/3 scaling appears only within bubble wake regions. Comparison with matched single-bubble DNS further indicates that the K41 scaling observed in the full BIT field arises from the superposition of individual bubble wakes. On this basis, we derive a scaling for the dissipation rate in dilute BIT that is consistent with experimental results from the literature.

</details>


### [115] [Data-Driven Surrogate Modeling for Micromixing of Non-Newtonian Fluids in Sinusoidal Converging-Diverging Microchannels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08547)
*Kritagya Sharma,Bimalendu Mahapatra*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文针对非牛顿流体（Carreau-Yasuda流体）在二维正弦收敛-扩张微通道中的被动微混合问题，通过高保真有限体积模拟系统研究了壁面振幅比、相位偏移和波数对混合性能的影响，并构建了以高斯过程回归（GPR）为核心的代理模型，结合NSGA-II多目标优化算法，在混合效率与压降之间寻得Pareto最优解，为被动微混合器的多目标设计提供了快速、准确且物理一致的机器学习框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非牛顿流体在微观尺度下的混合面临挑战，因为层流条件限制了横向输运主要依赖于分子扩散。被动微混合器的设计需要在混合性能与水力阻力之间权衡，而传统试错法或全保真仿真在多维设计空间中的探索效率极低，亟需一种高效的多目标设计方法。

Method: 采用高保真有限体积方法对Carreau-Yasuda流体在正弦收敛-扩张微通道中的蠕变流动进行系统参数化仿真（改变壁面振幅比、相位偏移和波数）；基于仿真数据构建多种代理模型，评估后选定GPR作为混合指数和压降的最准确预测器；将GPR与NSGA-II耦合进行多目标优化，求解Pareto前沿。

Result: 连续收缩-扩张单元通过界面拉伸、剪切率升高和剪切变稀引起的黏度降低三者的协同作用增强混合，但同时导致压降增大。GPR被确定为混合指数和压降预测精度最高的代理模型；GPR与NSGA-II耦合能够准确复现高保真仿真得到的Pareto前沿，识别出兼顾混合增强与压降降低的最优微通道几何构型。

Conclusion: 提出的机器学习代理框架为非牛顿流体被动微混合器提供了一种快速、准确且物理一致的多目标设计策略，有效解决了混合性能与水力阻力之间的内在权衡问题。

Abstract: Micromixing of non-Newtonian fluids remains challenging because laminar flow at microscales restricts transverse transport primarily to molecular diffusion. In this study, we investigate the transport mechanisms governing passive micromixing of a Carreau--Yasuda fluid in two-dimensional sinusoidal converging--diverging microchannels and develop a surrogate-assisted framework for their multi-objective design. We perform high-fidelity finite-volume simulations by systematically varying the wall-amplitude ratio, phase offset, and wave count under creeping-flow conditions. The results show that successive contraction--expansion units enhance mixing through the combined effects of interface stretching, elevated shear rates, and shear-thinning-induced viscosity reduction. These mechanisms improve scalar transport but simultaneously increase pressure drop, creating an inherent trade-off between mixing performance and hydraulic resistance. To efficiently explore the multidimensional design space, we construct surrogate models from high-fidelity numerical simulations and identify Gaussian Process Regression (GPR) as the most accurate predictor of both the mixing index and pressure drop. Coupling the validated GPR surrogate with Non-dominated Sorting Genetic Algorithm II (NSGA-II) accurately reproduces the Pareto front obtained from the high-fidelity simulations and identifies optimal microchannel geometries that balance mixing enhancement against pressure loss. The proposed machine learning framework provides a fast, accurate, and physically consistent strategy for the multi-objective design of passive micromixers for non-Newtonian fluids.

</details>


### [116] [Deformation dynamics of Oldroyd B drop in alternating electric field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08699)
*Sarika Shivaji Bangar,Gaurav Tomar*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过渐近分析与Basilisk直接数值模拟，研究了Oldroyd-B黏弹性液滴在均匀交变电场中的变形动力学，揭示了六种电水动力区域及不同频率下的变形模式与破裂行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黏弹性液滴在交变电场中的变形行为对微流控、喷墨打印和液滴操控等电水动力应用至关重要，但以往研究多集中于稳态电场，对交变电场下黏弹性效应的理解尚不充分，需要系统的理论分析与数值验证。

Method: 采用渐近分析（适用于小变形和弱弹性）推导Oldroyd-B液滴在Oldroyd-B介质中、泄漏电介质模型下轴对称Stokes流的解析解，并结合开源求解器Basilisk进行直接数值模拟以验证和扩展解析结果。

Result: 根据电导率和介电常数比识别出六种电水动力区域，表现出不同的变形模式、流动方向和非线性响应。高频下变形以两倍场频振荡；在产生长球形变的情形下，高频保持长球、中频出现短暂扁球偏移、低频出现大幅振荡；在产生扁球形变的情形下，高频保持扁球、中频出现凹陷、低频大De和Ca_E时破裂。

Conclusion: 交变电场频率和黏弹性共同决定了液滴的变形均值和振荡幅度，均值变形随De的变化呈单调或非单调行为，具体取决于频率和电学性质比；研究为黏弹性液滴在交变电场中的操控提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The deformation of viscoelastic drops in alternating electric fields is relevant to electrohydrodynamic applications such as microfluidics, inkjet printing, and drop manipulation. We investigate the dynamics of a neutrally buoyant Oldroyd-B drop subjected to a uniform alternating electric field using asymptotic analysis and direct numerical simulations with the open-source solver Basilisk. An analytical solution is derived for small deformation and weak elasticity for an Oldroyd-B drop suspended in an Oldroyd-B medium, with both fluids modeled as leaky dielectrics under axisymmetric Stokes flow. Depending on the conductivity and permittivity ratios, six electrohydrodynamic regions are identified, characterized by distinct deformation modes, flow directions, and nonlinear responses; while some exhibit stable spheroidal deformation, others undergo pointed-tip, multi-lobed, or oblate breakup beyond a critical electric capillary number. Across high-, intermediate-, and low-frequency regimes, the deformation oscillates at twice the applied field frequency, with its mean and amplitude governed by the field frequency and viscoelasticity. For cases that produce prolate deformation under a steady field, the drop remains prolate at high frequency, exhibits brief oblate excursions at intermediate frequency, and undergoes large-amplitude oscillations between near-spherical and highly deformed states at low frequency. The mean deformation varies monotonically or non-monotonically with $De$, depending on frequency and electrical property ratios. For cases producing oblate deformation under a steady field, the drop remains oblate at high frequency, develops dimples at intermediate frequency, and breaks up at low frequency for sufficiently large $De$ and $Ca_E$; the mean deformation increases monotonically with $De$.

</details>


### [117] [Turbulence anisotropy in a bubbly vertical channel flow with topological change](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08763)
*Arturo A. Arosemena,Davide Procacci,Simone Di Giorgio,Jannike Solsvik,Shahab Mirjalili*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过高保真数值模拟研究了向上流动的含气泡垂直通道流中，表面张力对载流体湍流各向异性的影响。研究发现大气泡尾流增强速度脉动，而多元相流在大多数区域比相同摩擦雷诺数的单相流更各向同性，这归因于大气泡对扫掠事件（高速流体向壁面运动）的增强促进了能量再分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索含气泡的垂直湍流通道流中表面张力对载流体湍流各向异性的影响，理解气泡拓扑变化（破裂与聚并）如何调制湍流结构及各向异性特征，揭示多元相流与单相流在湍流各向异性上的差异及其物理机制。

Method: 采用高保真数值模拟方法，对向上构型的含气泡垂直通道流进行模拟，其中气泡经历拓扑变化（破裂与聚并）。通过定性分析观测速度脉动增强现象，利用速度谱分析湍流结构的尺度特征，并采用重心图（barycentric map）及其他湍流各向异性指标定量评估载流体湍流的各向异性程度。

Result: (1) 大气泡尾流区域的载流体速度脉动显著增强；(2) 速度谱显示这些湍流结构以气泡尺寸为尺度，并与近壁区域的湍流结构相互作用；(3) 重心图和各向异性指标表明，除通道核心区（最大气泡所在区域）外，多元相流比相同摩擦雷诺数的单相流更各向同性；(4) 这一反常行为归因于大气泡增强扫掠事件，促进能量的更好再分配。

Conclusion: 大气泡的存在通过增强扫掠事件改善了湍流能量的再分配，从而使多元相流动在大部分区域内比匹配摩擦雷诺数的单相流更加各向同性，而非更各向异性。这一发现挑战了直觉上"气泡加入会使湍流更复杂/更各向异性"的预期，揭示了气泡对湍流各向异性调制的非直觉机制。

Abstract: High-fidelity numerical simulations of bubble-laden, vertical channel flow in the upward configuration, where the bubbles undergo topological changes (breakup and coalescence), were performed with the purpose of exploring the effect of the surface tension on the turbulence anisotropy in the carrier phase. A qualitative analysis shows that velocity fluctuations are enhanced in the wake of large bubbles. Moreover, as shown by the velocity spectra, these structures seem to scale with bubble size and interact with those closer to the wall. Finally, a barycentric map and other indicators of turbulence anisotropy clarify that, except at the core of the channel where the largest bubbles reside, the multiphase flow cases are actually more isotropic than the single-phase flow at a matching friction Reynolds number. This unexpected behavior is attributed to a better redistribution of energy due to an enhancement of sweep events (high-speed fluid towards the wall) in the presence of large bubbles.

</details>


### [118] [Exact solution for the motion of a rigid particle with $\boldsymbol{S_4}$ and $\boldsymbol{C_{2v}}$ symmetry settling under gravity in a viscous fluid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09585)
*Piotr Zdybel,Maria L. Ekiel-Jeżewska*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文对具有C₂ᵥ和S₄对称性的均匀密度刚体颗粒在低雷诺数粘性流体中重力沉降问题给出了精确且完备的解析解。S₄对称性使问题完全可积，所有轨道由单个守恒量0≤C≤1标记。研究发现0<C<1时存在两个时间尺度导致准周期演化，通过Jacobi椭圆函数和第三类不完全椭圆积分给出了全部三个欧拉角的闭合解，并获得了垂直和水平方向的位移解析表达式。水平运动轨迹呈玫瑰状开放曲线，被两个同心包络圆限定，作者给出了包络圆半径的简单精确表达式并证明了通约性条件可产生严格周期的玫瑰线轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非球对称颗粒在低雷诺数粘性流体中重力沉降的动力学行为是颗粒输运、化工和地质等领域的核心问题。以往对非轴对称颗粒沉降的研究多限于数值模拟或近似方法，缺乏对具有特定对称性的颗粒问题的精确解析描述。本文旨在利用S₄对称性提供的可积结构，给出该类颗粒沉降问题的完整解析解，从而深入揭示旋转-平动耦合、周期性和准周期性运动等基本规律。

Method: 本文采用解析方法：利用S₄对称性将运动方程简化为完全可积系统。将倾斜角θ和自转角ψ表示为时间的Jacobi椭圆周期函数；方位角φ通过具有复数特征参数的第三类不完全椭圆积分给出，并分解为均匀漂移项与严格周期调制项之和。竖直方向质心位移由时间的周期不完全椭圆积分表达，轨道平均沉降速度化简为两个完全椭圆积分的比值。水平方向位移通过三个欧拉角的代数组合精确获得，并推导出包络圆半径的闭合表达式及严格周期玫瑰线的通约性条件。

Result: 获得了颗粒全部分针运动（三个欧拉角及质心位移）的精确解析解。证明了0<C<1时系统具有两个时间尺度，导致准周期演化与显著的水平时变位移。水平轨迹呈玫瑰状开放曲线，由两个同心包络圆界定，包络圆半径具有极简的精确表达式，且在旋转-平动耦合趋于零的一族形状下趋于无穷。给出了轨迹上尖点产生的原因以及严格周期玫瑰线的通约性条件。

Conclusion: 本文为S₄对称性刚体颗粒的低雷诺数沉降问题提供了完整的精确解析框架，展示了对称性导致的完全可积性如何简化复杂的旋转-平动耦合动力学。所得结果不仅揭示了准周期运动、包络圆限界和玫瑰状轨迹等丰富现象，也为研究更一般对称性颗粒的沉降行为提供了可对照的基准解。

Abstract: We provide an exact and complete solution for the dynamics of a rigid particle of uniform density with $C_{2v}$ and $S_4$ symmetry, settling under gravity in a viscous fluid at a Reynolds number much smaller than unity. The $S_4$ symmetry renders the problem exactly integrable, with all orbits labelled by a single conserved quantity $0\le C\le 1$. We show that, for $0<C<1$, there are two different time scales, which lead to quasi-periodic evolution, with a significant time-dependent horizontal displacement. We obtain the tilt $θ$ and spin $ψ$ Euler angles as periodic Jacobi elliptic functions of time, and the azimuthal angle $φ$ through an incomplete elliptic integral of the third kind with a complex characteristic, which splits $φ$ into a uniform drift and a strictly periodic modulation. The orientation period and the drift rate (corresponding to a constant angular velocity around the gravity direction) follow in a closed form. The vertical centre-of-mass displacement is obtained in terms of periodic in time incomplete elliptic integrals, and the periodic orbit-averaged settling velocity reduces to a single ratio of complete elliptic integrals. The horizontal component of the motion in the laboratory frame of reference is obtained exactly and algebraically in terms of all three Euler angles, so it is quasi-periodic. The horizontal component of the centre-of-mass position traces rosette-like, in general open curves confined by two concentric `envelope' circles. We determine very simple exact expressions for radii of both envelopes and demonstrate that they tend to infinity for a family of shapes with the rotation-translation coupling decreasing to zero. We also explain the origin of cusps at the rosette-like trajectory and provide a commensurability condition selecting strictly periodic rosettes.

</details>


### [119] [Memory-Generated Transport Geometry: Curvature, Holonomy, and Irreversibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09607)
*Mounir Kassmi*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个全新的理论框架，将有限记忆视为输运几何结构的生成器而非修正项。通过从因果输运历史重构变形，瞬时速度梯度被记忆依赖的输运联络所取代，其有序演化产生非交换性、曲率和完整（holonomy）。即使在时间周期性、无旋流动中，有限记忆也能产生非零的几何输运贡献，为不可逆拉格朗日输运提供了纯运动学机制，无需依赖涡度、本构非线性、随机强迫或显式对称破缺。框架预测了由无量纲参数(ωτ_m)控制的普适标度律、特征记忆尺度(τ_c)以及单调递减的记忆磁化率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统输运理论将记忆作为已给定运动学结构上的本构修正，本文旨在反转这一范式，建立有限记忆本身生成输运几何结构的新框架，从而解释不可逆拉格朗日输运的深层机制。

Method: 从因果输运历史重构变形，将瞬时速度梯度替换为记忆依赖的输运联络；通过联络的有序演化引出非交换性、曲率和完整；引入与输运表示无关的内在曲率不变量；使用无量纲参数(ωτ_m)进行标度分析，并通过数值模拟验证理论预测。

Result: 有限记忆在时间周期性无旋流动中产生非零的几何输运贡献；识别出特征记忆尺度(τ_c)区分快速几何累积与渐近饱和；记忆磁化率单调递减且累积动力学全局凹；数值模拟证实几何不可逆性通过渐进曲率累积而非共振放大涌现。

Conclusion: 有限记忆是内在几何结构的生成器而非动力演化的修饰者；因果历史是曲率、完整性和不可逆输运的微观起源，适用于广泛的非马尔可夫系统。

Abstract: Memory is traditionally incorporated into transport theory as a constitutive correction acting on an already prescribed kinematic structure. Here we develop a different framework in which finite memory itself generates the geometry of transport. By reconstructing deformation from causal transport histories, the instantaneous velocity gradient is replaced by a memory-dependent transport connection whose ordered evolution gives rise to noncommutativity, curvature, and holonomy in transport-history space. We show that finite memory generates a nonvanishing geometric contribution to transport even in time-periodic, irrotational flows, providing a purely kinematic mechanism for irreversible Lagrangian transport without invoking vorticity, constitutive nonlinearities, stochastic forcing, or explicit symmetry breaking. We introduce an intrinsic curvature invariant, independent of the transport representation that measures the accumulated geometric structure generated by transport history. The framework predicts universal scaling governed by the dimensionless parameter (ωτ_m), identifies a characteristic memory scale (tau_c) separating rapid geometric accumulation from asymptotic saturation, and reveals a monotonically decreasing memory susceptibility with globally concave accumulation dynamics. Numerical simulations confirm these predictions and show that geometric irreversibility emerges through progressive curvature accumulation rather than resonance-driven amplification. These results establish finite memory as a generator of an intrinsic geometric structure rather than merely a modifier of dynamical evolution, revealing causal history as the microscopic origin of curvature, holonomy, and irreversible transport across a broad class of non-Markovian systems.

</details>
